\|\cdot\|_{\frac{q}{q-1}}\leq\|\cdot\|^{\theta}_{\frac{N+p}{N}}\|\cdot\|^{1-\theta}_{1}

with
\theta=\frac{N+p}{pq}
. After that we apply Young’s inequality to obtain for every
\varepsilon>0
that

(3.7)

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{T}}}

\displaystyle\leq\varepsilon\bigg[\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big(\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)\zeta^{p}\big)^{\frac{N+p}{N}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\Big]^{\frac{N}{N+p}}

\displaystyle\qquad+\varepsilon^{-\frac{N+p}{qp-(N+p)}}\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}\zeta^{p}\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Using again that
\frac{q}{q-1}<\frac{N+p}{N}<{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}\frac{p+2\alpha}{2p\alpha}
, we first apply Hölder’s inequality to estimate the first integral in (
3.7
) (noting again
\varrho\leq 1
) and then apply (
3.6
) to get

\displaystyle\bigg[\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\big(\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)\zeta^{p}\big)^{\frac{N+p}{N}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{N}{N+p}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big(\zeta^{p}\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)\big)^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}\frac{p+2\alpha}{2p\alpha}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{2p\alpha}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}(p+2\alpha)}}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\Big(\zeta^{\frac{p+2\alpha}{2\alpha}}\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)^{\frac{p+2\alpha}{2p\alpha}}\Big)^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{2p\alpha}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}(p+2\alpha)}}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\Big(\zeta\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)^{\frac{p+2\alpha}{2p\alpha}}\Big)^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{2p\alpha}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}(p+2\alpha)}}

\displaystyle\leq C{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}^{\frac{N+p}{N}\frac{2p\alpha}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}(p+2\alpha)}}.

With the above estimate at hand, we plug (
3.7
) into the energy estimate to get

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\alpha)^{p}(\varrho^{-p}+\|\nabla\zeta\|^{p}_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}\zeta\|_{\infty})\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big[|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1\big]\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C(1+\alpha)^{p^{\prime}+p}\varepsilon{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}^{\frac{N+p}{N}\frac{2p\alpha}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}(p+2\alpha)}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\varepsilon^{-\frac{N+p}{qp-(N+p)}}(1+\alpha)^{p^{\prime}+p}\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\zeta^{p}\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

In the above estimate, we further use the fact that
{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}^{\frac{N+p}{N}\frac{2p\alpha}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}(p+2\alpha)}}\leq{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}+1
since the power is in
(0,1)
and then choose
\varepsilon
to satisfy
C(1+\alpha)^{p^{\prime}+p}\varepsilon=\frac{1}{2}
to absorb
{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}
to the left-hand side.
Moreover, using this choice of
\varepsilon
and the fact that
\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)\leq|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1
, the previous inequality eventually gives that

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{L}}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\alpha)^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}(\varrho^{-p}+\|\nabla\zeta\|^{p}_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}\zeta\|_{\infty})\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big[|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1\big]\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Plug this into (
3.6
) to get

\displaystyle\bigg[\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\Big(\zeta\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)^{\frac{p+2\alpha}{2p\alpha}}\Big)^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{N}{N+p}}

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\alpha)^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}(\varrho^{-p}+\|\nabla\zeta\|^{p}_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}\zeta\|_{\infty})\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big[|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1\big]\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Note that the right-hand side is finite and independent of
k
and
\ell
. Moreover,

\lim_{\ell\to\infty}\lim_{k\downarrow 0}\Phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}\big(|u|^{2}\big)^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}\,\frac{p+2\alpha}{2p\alpha}}=|u|^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{p}(p+2\alpha)}.

Since
0\leq\zeta\leq 1
and
\phi_{\alpha,\ell,k}(|u|^{2})\uparrow|u|^{2\alpha}
as
k\downarrow 0
,
\ell\to\infty
, we may pass to the limit in the previous estimate by monotone convergence to obtain

\displaystyle\bigg(\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}

\displaystyle\zeta^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,|u|^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{p}(p+2\alpha)}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg)^{\!\frac{N}{N+p}}

(3.8)

\displaystyle\leq C(1+\alpha)^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{\,1-\frac{N+p}{qp}\,}}(\varrho^{-p}+\|\nabla\zeta\|^{p}_{\infty}+\|\partial_{t}\zeta\|_{\infty})\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big(|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

where
C=C(N,p,C_{o},C_{1})
, and
{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}
is defined in (
3.5
).

By a standard covering argument, the estimate on

Q_{\frac{1}{2}\varrho}
propagates to compactly contained cylinders. Consequently,

|u|\in L^{2+2\alpha}_{\rm loc}(E_{T})\quad\Longrightarrow\quad|u|\in L^{(p+2\alpha)\frac{\bm{\mathfrak{q}}}{p}}_{\rm loc}(E_{T}).

In order to have

(p+2\alpha)\frac{\bm{\mathfrak{q}}}{p}>2+2\alpha,

we need

N(p-2)+(2+2\alpha)p>0,

and this is guaranteed by the existence of
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}>2
such that
\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}>0
; indeed, it is enough to let
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\equiv 2+2\alpha
.

Step 2. Setup of the iteration.

Fix
Q_{o}:=Q_{\varrho}\subset E_{T}
and define a shrinking family of cylinders

Q_{i}:=Q_{\varrho_{i}}=B_{\varrho_{i}}\times(-\varrho_{i}^{\,p},0]
,
i\in\mathbb{N}_{0}
,
and radii

\varrho_{i}=\frac{\varrho}{2}+\frac{\varrho}{2^{i+1}},\quad\mbox{for $i\in\mathbb{N}_{0}$.}

Then
(\varrho_{i})_{i\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}

is decreasing,
\varrho_{o}=\varrho
, and
\varrho_{\infty}=\frac{1}{2}\varrho
. For each
i
choose
\zeta=\zeta_{i}\in W^{1,\infty}(Q_{i},[0,1])
such that
\zeta\equiv 1
on
Q_{i+1}
,
\zeta=0
on
\partial_{\rm par}Q_{i}
, and

|\nabla\zeta|\leq\frac{2^{i+2}}{\varrho},\quad\mbox{and}\quad|\partial_{t}\zeta|\leq\frac{2^{i+4}}{\varrho^{p}}.

Apply the above choice of
\zeta=\zeta_{i}
in (
3.2
) and take integral averages
yield that

\displaystyle\bigg[

\displaystyle\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i+1}}|u|^{(p+2\alpha)\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{p}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{N}{N+p}}

\displaystyle\leq C\,(1+\alpha)^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}\bigg[1+\varrho^{p}\Big(\frac{2^{p(i+2)}}{\varrho^{p}}+\frac{2^{i+4}}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)\bigg]\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i}}\big(|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(3.9)

\displaystyle\leq C\,2^{pi}(1+\alpha)^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i}}\big(|u|^{2+2\alpha}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Let
\kappa:=1+\tfrac{p}{N}
. A direct calculation shows

(p+2\alpha)\,\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{p}=2\alpha\,\kappa\;+\;p\,\frac{N+2}{N}.

Therefore, it is natural to choose
\alpha_{o}
by
2\alpha_{o}+2={\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
, and to define
(\alpha_{i})_{i\in\mathbb{N}_{0}}
recursively by

2\alpha_{i+1}+2=2\kappa\,\alpha_{i}\;+\;p\,\frac{N+2}{N}.

Equivalently, with
s_{i}:=2+2\alpha_{i}
one has the linear recurrence

s_{i+1}=\kappa s_{i}+(p-2),\qquad s_{o}={\bm{\mathsf{r}}}.

Hence,

\displaystyle s_{i}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{i}{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+\frac{p-2}{\kappa-1}(\kappa^{i}-1)

\displaystyle=\kappa^{i}\Big({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+\frac{N}{p}(p-2)\Big)-\frac{N}{p}(p-2)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\kappa^{i}-\frac{1}{p}\bm{\lambda}_{2}+2,

so that
s_{i}\to\infty
as
i\to\infty
. In particular, the iteration (
3.2
) starts with the
L^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
-integral of
|u|
on
Q_{o}
on the right-hand side, which is finite by assumption, and produces a strictly improving sequence of integrability exponents.
Inserting the recursion

2\alpha_{i+1}+2=2\kappa\,\alpha_{i}\;+\;p\,\frac{N+2}{N}

into (
3.2
) yields

\displaystyle\bigg[\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i+1}}|u|^{2+2\alpha_{i+1}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{N}{N+p}}\leq C\,2^{pi}(1+\alpha_{i})^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i}}\big(|u|^{2+2\alpha_{i}}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

In order to control
1+\alpha_{i}
in terms of
\kappa^{i}
, note that with
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=N(p-2)+p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
and
\bm{\lambda}_{2}=N(p-2)+2p
we have

\displaystyle s_{i}=2+2\alpha_{i}

\displaystyle=2+\frac{1}{p}\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\kappa^{i}-\frac{1}{p}\bm{\lambda}_{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{p}\,N(2-p)+\frac{1}{p}\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,\kappa^{i}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{p}\,[\,N(2-p)+\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,]\,\kappa^{i}={\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,\kappa^{i}.

Consequently,

1+\alpha_{i}\;\leq\;\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{2}\,\kappa^{i}.

Substituting this inequality
into the preceding estimate, we obtain the
recursive inequality

(3.10)

\displaystyle\bigg[\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i+1}}|u|^{2+2\alpha_{i+1}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{N}{N+p}}\leq C\,\Big(2^{p}\kappa^{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}\Big)^{i}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i}}\big(|u|^{2+2\alpha_{i}}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

where now
C
takes into account also the term
\left(\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{2}\right)^{{\frac{p^{\prime}+p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}}
.
In order to complete the iteration, we add
1
on the left side integral and introduce

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}=\bigg[\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{i}}\big(|u|^{s_{i}}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{s_{i}}},\qquad{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}=2^{p}\kappa^{\frac{p^{\prime}p}{1-\frac{N+p}{qp}}}.

In this notation, (
3.10
) takes the form

{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i+1}^{s_{i+1}}\leq\big(C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{i}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}^{s_{i}}\big)^{\kappa},\quad\forall\,i\in\mathbb{N}_{0},

and the iteration yields

{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}\leq\prod_{j=1}^{i}C^{\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{s_{i}}}\prod_{j=1}^{i}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{j\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{s_{i}}}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}^{\frac{s_{o}\kappa^{i}}{s_{i}}},\qquad\forall\,i\in\mathbb{N}_{0}.

Unwinding the products gives

{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}\leq C^{\frac{1}{s_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\kappa^{\,i-j+1}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{\frac{1}{s_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{i}j\kappa^{i-j+1}}\;{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}^{\frac{s_{o}\kappa^{i}}{s_{i}}}.

Recall that
s_{o}=2\alpha_{o}+2={\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
. To compute and bound the exponents, note that

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{s_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{2\alpha_{i}+2}=\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}\kappa^{i}-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{2}}{p}+2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}\kappa^{i}+\frac{N(2-p)}{p}}\leq\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\kappa^{-j+1},

and likewise

\displaystyle\frac{s_{o}\kappa^{i}}{s_{i}}

\displaystyle=\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}\kappa^{i}+\frac{N(2-p)}{p}}\kappa^{i}\leq\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}}=\frac{p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}.

Here we used
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=N(p-2)+{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}p
and
\bm{\lambda}_{2}=N(p-2)+2p
, so that
2p-\bm{\lambda}_{2}=N(2-p)
. In particular, we obtain the bound

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{i}\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{s_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\sum_{j=1}^{i}\kappa^{-j+1}\leq\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\frac{1}{1-1/\kappa}=\frac{N+p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}},

and, similarly,

\displaystyle\sum_{j=1}^{i}j\frac{\kappa^{i-j+1}}{s_{i}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\sum_{j=1}^{i}j\kappa^{-j+1}\leq\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\Big(\frac{\kappa}{\kappa-1}\Big)^{2}=\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\Big(\frac{N+p}{p}\Big)^{2}.

Using these estimates to control the exponents in the product representation of
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}
yields

\limsup_{i\rightarrow\infty}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}\leq C^{\frac{N+p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}(\frac{N+p}{p})^{2}}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}^{\frac{p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}.

Moreover, straightforward computations give that

\displaystyle\limsup_{i\rightarrow\infty}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{i}\geq\sup_{Q_{\frac{1}{2}\varrho}}|u|,\qquad{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}^{\frac{p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}

\displaystyle=\bigg[\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big(|u|^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}.

Therefore, we have

\sup_{Q_{\frac{1}{2}\varrho}}|u|\leq\Bigg[C\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho}}\big(|u|^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+1\big)\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\Bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}},

where
C
depends on
N,p,C_{o},C_{1},q,{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
and also on
\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{1}}
and
\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{1}}
. As a result of a standard covering argument, we conclude that
u
is locally bounded in
E_{T}
.
∎

Remark 3.5
.

It is straightforward to verify that if
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=\frac{N(2-p)}{p}
, that is, if
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=0
, then
\forall i\in{\mathbb{N}}_{0}
we have
s_{i}={\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
, and the iteration process described above cannot converge. This does not exclude the possibility that
u
is bounded; however, such a conclusion cannot be obtained by our method, and therefore remains an open problem.

Remark 3.6

(Qualitative
L^{\infty}
-estimate for
\frac{2N}{N+2}<p\leq 2
)
.

In this range we assume

u\in C^{0}\big([0,T];L^{2}(E,\mathbb{R}^{k})\big)\cap L^{p}\big(0,T;W^{1,p}(E,\mathbb{R}^{k})\big).

By the parabolic Sobolev embedding,

u\in L^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}_{\rm loc}(E_{T},\mathbb{R}^{k})\quad\text{with}\quad{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=p\,\frac{N+2}{N}>2.

In particular,
u\in L^{2}_{\rm loc}(E_{T},\mathbb{R}^{k})
. Moreover,

\bm{\lambda}_{2}:=N(p-2)+2p>0\qquad\text{whenever}\quad p>\tfrac{2N}{N+2}.

Hence the structural condition
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}>0
is satisfied,
taking
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=2
. Consequently, the argument developed for the sub-critical case
1<p\leq\tfrac{2N}{N+2}
applies verbatim (with this choice of
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
) and yields the local boundedness of
u
without any extra assumption.

3.3.
De Giorgi’s iteration: proof of Theorem
1.1

Throughout the proof the center point

z_{o}
of all cylinders is fixed, without loss of generality we may assume it to coincide with the origin
(0,0)
, and we suppress it in the notation. Since by Lemma
3.3
weak solutions are locally bounded, the energy estimate in Lemma
3.2
is at our disposal.
In fact, Lemma
3.2
gives

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(-\theta,0]}\int_{B_{\varrho}\times\{t\}}v\zeta^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}\Big[|u|^{p}|\nabla\zeta|^{p}+\mu^{p}\zeta^{p}\Big]\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t+C\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}v|\partial_{t}\zeta^{p}|\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\leq\,}+C\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{p}|u|^{p^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\}}\zeta^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t+\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}||u|^{p^{\prime}+1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\}}\zeta^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

where
C=C(p,C_{o},C_{1})
,
p^{\prime}=\frac{p}{p-1}
, and

\tfrac{1}{2+p^{\prime}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\leq v=\int_{0}^{|u|}s(s-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}})_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq\tfrac{1}{2+p^{\prime}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\}}.

This energy estimate holds for every cylinder
Q_{\varrho,\theta}\Subset E_{T}
, for every level
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\geq 0
, and for every cut-off
\zeta\in W^{1,\infty}(Q_{\varrho,\theta},[0,1])
vanishing on the parabolic boundary
\partial_{\rm par}Q_{\varrho,\theta}
. Fix a cylinder
Q_{o}:=Q_{\varrho,\theta}\Subset E_{T}
and choose
0<\tau<1
. Define a nested family of cylinders

Q_{n}:=Q_{\varrho_{n},\theta_{n}}
with

\varrho_{n}:=\tau\varrho+\frac{1-\tau}{2^{\,n}}\varrho,\qquad\theta_{n}:=\tau\theta+\frac{1-\tau}{2^{\,n}}\theta,\qquad n\in\mathbb{N}_{0},

so that
Q_{n+1}\Subset Q_{n}
,
\varrho_{n}\downarrow\tau\varrho
, and
\theta_{n}\downarrow\tau\theta
.
For each
n
pick
\zeta_{n}\in W^{1,\infty}(Q_{n},[0,1])
with

\zeta_{n}\equiv 1
on
Q_{n+1}
,

\zeta_{n}=0
on
\partial_{\rm par}Q_{n}
,

|\nabla\zeta_{n}|\leq\frac{2^{n+2}}{(1-\tau)\varrho},\quad\mbox{and}\quad|\partial_{t}\zeta_{n}|\leq\frac{2^{n+2}}{(1-\tau)\theta}.

The truncation levels are taken increasing to
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}>0
by

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}:={\bm{\mathsf{k}}}-\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}}{2^{n}},

so that
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}=\tfrac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}}{2^{n+1}}
and
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\uparrow{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
. With these preparations, we insert the pairs
(\zeta_{n},{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1})
in the above energy inequality on the cylinders
Q_{n}
and obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}

\displaystyle\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)^{p^{\prime}+2}\zeta_{n}^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq C2^{(n+1)p}\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{1}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\varrho^{p}}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{2}}{(1-\tau)\theta}\bigg]+C\big[{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{3}+{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{4}+{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{5}\big],

where we abbreviated

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{1}

\displaystyle:=\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+p}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{2}

\displaystyle:=\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{3}

\displaystyle:=\mu^{p}\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{p^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{4}

\displaystyle:=\iint_{Q_{n}}|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{p}|u|^{p^{\prime}}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{5}

\displaystyle:=\iint_{Q_{n}}|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}||u|^{p^{\prime}+1}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Our next aim is to dominate each term on the right-hand side of the energy inequality by a truncation integral
\|(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n})_{+}\|_{a,Q_{n}}
with the same exponent
a
.
For this purpose, we first establish two estimates that will be used repeatedly: For
a\geq b>0
we have

(3.11)

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\leq\frac{2^{(n+1)a}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{a-b}}\iint_{Q_{n}}

\displaystyle\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{a}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

and

(3.12)

\displaystyle\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|\leq\Big(\frac{2^{n+1}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}}\Big)^{a}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{a}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

To show the first estimate, we use the relation between consecutive levels,

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}={\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\frac{2^{n+1}-2}{2^{n+1}-1},

and compute

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n}}

\displaystyle\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{a}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\geq\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{b}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{a-b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq\big({\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)^{a-b}\iint_{Q_{n}}\bigg[|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\frac{2^{n+1}-2}{2^{n+1}-1}\bigg]^{b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}}{2^{n+1}}\Big)^{a-b}\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{b}\bigg[1-\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}}{|u|}\frac{2^{n+1}-2}{2^{n+1}-1}\bigg]^{b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq\Big(\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}}{2^{n+1}}\Big)^{a-b}\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{b}\bigg[1-\frac{2^{n+1}-2}{2^{n+1}-1}\bigg]^{b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{a-b}}{2^{(n+1)a}}\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{b}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

To prove the second estimate, we compute

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{a}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{a}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\geq\big({\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)^{a}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|

\displaystyle=\Big(\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}}{2^{n+1}}\Big)^{a}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|.

Since
1<p\leq\frac{2N}{N+2}
, the condition
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=N(p-2)+p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}>0
forces

{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}>\frac{N(2-p)}{p}\geq 2
. To proceed, the decisive point is the comparison of
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
with
p^{\prime}+2
.

In the
case

\bm{2\,\leq\frac{N(2-p)}{p}}<{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2

we will dominate each term on the right-hand side of the energy inequality in terms of the
L^{p^{\prime}+2}
-integral of the truncated function
(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n})_{+}
.
Using (
3.11
) with
(a,b)=(p^{\prime}+2,p^{\prime}+p)
we estimate the term
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{1}
as

(3.13)

{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{1}\leq\frac{2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2-p}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t=2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+p}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n},

where we set

\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}:=\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Similarly, the
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{2}
-term is estimated by applying (
3.11
) with
a=b=p^{\prime}+2
as

(3.14)

{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{2}\leq 2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t=2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}.

Applying (
3.12
) with
a=p^{\prime}+2
we have

\displaystyle\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(3.15)

\displaystyle=2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}.

We now estimate the non–degeneracy term
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{3}
. By Hölder’s inequality, together with (
3.13
) and (
3.3
), we obtain

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{p}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+p}\mathbf{1}_{\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{p}\big[2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+p}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}\big]^{\frac{1}{p}}\big[2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}\widetilde{\bm{Y}}_{n}\big]^{1-\frac{1}{p}}

(3.16)

\displaystyle=\mu^{p}2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}.

In the same vein we estimate
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{4}
(the

{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}
-term). Using the local boundedness of
u
, Hölder’s inequality with exponents
q
and
\frac{q}{q-1}
and the measure bound (
3.3
), we obtain

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{4}

\displaystyle\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{o}}|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{q}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{1}{q}}

(3.17)

\displaystyle\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{p}2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)(1-\frac{1}{q})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}.

We now handle the
{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}
-term.
By the local boundedness of
u
, Hölder’s inequality with exponents
qp^{\prime}
,
(1-\frac{1}{qp^{\prime}})^{-1}
gives

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{5}

\displaystyle\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{qp^{\prime}}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{1}{qp^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{1}{q}+(\frac{1}{q}-\frac{1}{qp^{\prime}})}

\displaystyle\leq|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{qp}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{1}{q}}

(3.18)

\displaystyle\leq|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{qp}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)(1-\frac{1}{q})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}.

Here, we also used (
3.12
) with
a=p^{\prime}+2
in the last line.
Collecting the bounds
(
3.13
), (
3.14
), (
3.3
), (
3.3
), and (
3.3
), and inserting them into the energy inequality, we obtain for each
n\in\mathbb{N}_{0}

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\zeta_{n}^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\qquad\leq C\,2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+p+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\bigg[\frac{1}{(1-\tau)^{p}\varrho^{p}}\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2-p}}+\frac{1}{(1-\tau)\theta}+\frac{\mu^{p}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2}}\bigg]\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}

\displaystyle\qquad\phantom{\leq\,}+C\Big[\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{p}+|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{qp}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}\Big]2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)(1-\frac{1}{q})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\qquad\leq\frac{C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+p+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}\bigg[\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2-p}}\Big(1+\frac{\mu^{p}\varrho^{p}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p}}\Big)+1\bigg]\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}

\displaystyle\qquad\phantom{\leq\,}+C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)(1-\frac{1}{q})}{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}},

where

(3.19)

{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}:=\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{p}+|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{qp}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}.

We now set the first largeness requirements on
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
: indeed, we require

\frac{\mu^{p}\varrho^{p}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p}}\leq 1\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\geq\mu\varrho,

and

\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2-p}}\leq 1\quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\geq\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}}.

That is, taking

(3.20)

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}\geq\max\bigg\{\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}},\,\mu\varrho\bigg\}

the bracketed factors in front of
\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}
are bounded by
3
; we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}

\displaystyle\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)^{p^{\prime}+2}\zeta_{n}^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+p+2)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}+C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)(1-\frac{1}{q})}{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}

(3.21)

\displaystyle\leq C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+p+2)}\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}+{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}\bigg].

Next, we invoke the parabolic Sobolev embedding (Proposition 3.1 in Chapter I of
[
4
]
) with

(v,p,m,q)=\Big(\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}},p,{\bm{\mathsf{m}}},{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}\Big),

where

(3.22)

{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}:=\frac{p^{\prime}+2}{p^{\prime}},\qquad{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}:=p\frac{N+{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{N}.

This gives

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n}}

\displaystyle\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq C_{\rm Sob}^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]

(3.23)

\displaystyle\qquad\,\,\,\cdot\bigg[\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta^{p^{\prime}}_{n}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big[\mbox{right-hand side of \eqref{est:en-prel}}\Big]^{1+\frac{p}{N}}.

Observe that the hypothesis
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=N(p-2)+p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}>0
together with the small
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
-assumption
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2
, implies
{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}>{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}
. Indeed,

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{Np^{\prime}p+p(p^{\prime}+2)}{Np^{\prime}}=\frac{N(p+p^{\prime})+p(p^{\prime}+2)}{Np^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{N(p-2)+p(p^{\prime}+2)+N(p^{\prime}+2)}{Np^{\prime}}>\frac{N(p-2)+p{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+N(p^{\prime}+2)}{Np^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{Np^{\prime}}+\frac{p^{\prime}+2}{p^{\prime}}>{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}.

Consequently, Hölder’s inequality yields the integrability gain

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n+1}}

\displaystyle\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\leq\iint_{Q_{n}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{\bm{\mathfrak{m}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}}.

Let us continue to estimate the first term by (
3.3
) and the second term by (
3.3
). After a simple algebraic manipulation we get

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n+1}}

\displaystyle\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq C\Big[\mbox{right-hand side of \eqref{est:en-prel}}\Big]^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1+\frac{p}{N})}\Big[2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+2)}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}\Big]^{1-\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+p+2)(1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{\bm{\mathfrak{m}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}}\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}+{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}\bigg]^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1+\frac{p}{N})}

\displaystyle\leq C2^{(n+1)(p^{\prime}+p+2)(1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}})}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{(p^{\prime}+2)\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\frac{N+p}{N}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,\,\,}\cdot\bigg[\frac{\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{\frac{1}{q}}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}\bigg]^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\frac{N+p}{N}}.

Dividing both sides by
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}
changes the power of
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
on the right. Using
{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}=p\,\frac{N+{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{N}
and
{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}=\frac{p^{\prime}+2}{p^{\prime}}
, we simplify the power as

\displaystyle(p^{\prime}+2)\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\frac{N+p}{N}-(p^{\prime}+2)=(p^{\prime}+2)\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}(N+p)}{p(N+{\bm{\mathsf{m}}})}-1\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{p^{\prime}+2}{p(N+{\bm{\mathsf{m}}})}\bigg[{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}(N+p)-p\big(N+{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}\big)\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad=\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{p\frac{N+{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{N}}\big(p^{\prime}+2-pp^{\prime}\big)=\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(2-p),

where we used the identity
pp^{\prime}=p+p^{\prime}
. Hence, dividing by
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}

and collecting constants, we arrive at the recursive bound

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n+1}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(2-p)}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}\bigg[\frac{\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}^{q}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\frac{N+p}{qp}},

where

{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}:=2^{(p^{\prime}+p+2)(1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}})}.

Passing to mean values in
\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n+1}
,
\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}
, and
\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{o}

(and collecting the resulting volume factors) yields the recursive inequality

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n+1}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{p^{\prime}+2}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}p^{\prime}}(2-p)}|Q_{o}|^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,\,\,}\cdot\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}^{q}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\frac{N+p}{qp}},

where

{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}:=\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Here
{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}
abbreviates the contribution arising from the source terms in the previous step.

With a judicious choice of the truncation level

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
, the right–hand side can be streamlined. Indeed, the bracket splits into three contributions – coming from
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}
, the
{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}
–term, and the
{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}
–term, namely

\Big[\dots\Big]=\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{qp^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}+\frac{|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{p}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{q(p^{\prime}+1)}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}^{q}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}.

We now fix the truncation level
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}

so large that

(3.24)

\displaystyle\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}-q}(1-\tau)^{N+p-qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}},

\displaystyle\frac{\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{qp^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}-q}(1-\tau)^{N+p-qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}},

\displaystyle\frac{|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{p}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{q(p^{\prime}+1)}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}^{q}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}-q}(1-\tau)^{N+p-qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}}.

Recalling
|Q_{o}|=|B_{1}|\varrho^{N}\theta

and
\bm{\lambda}_{s}=N(p-2)+ps
, solving the first condition for
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
yields

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}{p}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{|Q_{o}|}{\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(3.25)

\displaystyle=\frac{|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Similarly, the second condition is ensured provided

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}{p}}

\displaystyle\geq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{qp^{\prime}}\theta^{q-\frac{N+p}{p}}|Q_{o}|\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(3.26)

\displaystyle=|B_{1}|\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{qp^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

The third one is satisfied provided

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}{p}}

\displaystyle\geq\theta^{q-\frac{N+p}{p}}|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{p}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{q(p^{\prime}+1)}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{o}}|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}

(3.27)

\displaystyle=|B_{1}|\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{q(p^{\prime}+1)}\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\bigg[\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}.

The first condition (
3.3
) is certainly satisfied if we choose

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
so that

(3.28)

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}{p}}\geq\frac{|B_{1}|\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+2-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

With
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}

fixed so that (
3.20
), (
3.28
), (
3.3
), and (
3.3
) all hold, the recursion collapses to

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}\Bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}|Q_{o}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\Bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}.

With

\alpha:=\frac{p}{N}\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\Big(1-\frac{N+p}{qp}\Big),\quad{\sf C}:=C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}\Bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}|Q_{o}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\Bigg]^{\alpha},

the recursion reads

{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n+1}\leq{\sf C}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}^{1+\alpha}.

By Lemma
2.2
,
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}\to 0
provided

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}

\displaystyle\leq{\sf C}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}}.

This is equivalent to

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{(C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}})^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}}}\frac{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}|Q_{o}|}.

Rearranging and recalling
\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}=N(p-2)+p(p^{\prime}+2)

yields the condition

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}{p}}={\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(p-2)+p^{\prime}+2}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{(C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{1+\frac{1}{\alpha}})^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}|Q_{o}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle=\frac{C|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

This is certainly ensured if we choose
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
so that

(3.29)

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}{p}}\geq\frac{C|B_{1}|\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+2-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Indeed, since
|u|^{p^{\prime}+2}\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+2-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}
on
Q_{o}
, the condition (
3.29
) implies
(
3.28
). In particular, because
C\geq 1
, (
3.29
) is strictly stronger than (
3.28
) and thus also guarantees the corresponding requirement used in the iteration.
By Lemma
2.2
, the recursion yields
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}\to{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{\infty}=0

as
n\to\infty
. Since
Q_{n}\downarrow Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}
and
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\uparrow{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
, this implies

\iint_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}})_{+}^{p^{\prime}+2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t=0,

and hence
(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}})_{+}=0
a.e. in the limit cylinder
Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}
. In other words,

(3.30)

\sup_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}|u|\leq{\bm{\mathsf{k}}},

provided
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
has been chosen large enough to satisfy (
3.20
), (
3.29
), (
3.3
), and (
3.3
).
With the preparatory bounds in place, we now fix the truncation level by

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}

\displaystyle:=\max\Bigg\{\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}}\bigg[\frac{C|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}}\bigg[|B_{1}|\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}}\bigg[|B_{1}|^{p^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}},

(3.31)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}},\,\mu\varrho\Bigg\}.

With
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
chosen as in (
3.3
), the conditions (
3.20
), (
3.29
), (
3.3
), and (
3.3
) are all satisfied, so the iteration applies and, by Lemma
2.2
,
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}\to 0
. Consequently, the conclusion (
3.30
) follows. This completes the De Giorgi iteration in the case
2<{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2
.

The bound (
3.30
) with (
3.3
) can be refined via an interpolation. To set up the interpolation, we analyze the first three entries in the maximum more closely. For the first entry, Young’s inequality with exponents

\big(1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}\big)^{-1}
and
\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}{\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}
yields

(3.32)

\displaystyle\big[\mbox{first entry}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{6}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}+C\bigg[\frac{|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}},

where the constant
C
in (
3.32
) denotes
6^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{p^{\prime}+2}}{\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}}C^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}}
, with
C
taken from (
3.3
).
For the second entry (the
{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}
-term), Young’s inequality with exponents

\big(1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\big)^{-1}
and

\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}
gives

(3.33)

\displaystyle\big[\mbox{second entry}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{6}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}+C\bigg[|B_{1}|\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}}.

For the third entry (the
{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}
-term), Young’s inequality with exponents

\big(1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\big)^{-1}

and
\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q}}
implies

(3.34)

\displaystyle\big[\mbox{third entry}\big]

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{6}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}+C\bigg[|B_{1}|^{p^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q}}}.

Therefore, for any
\tau\in[\frac{1}{2},1)
we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}|u|

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\sup_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|u|+\frac{C}{(1-\tau)^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}(N+p)}}\bigg[\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}}

(3.35)

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q}}}+\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}}+\mu\varrho.

Fix
\tau\in[\frac{1}{2},1)
and let
\tau\leq\tau_{1}<\tau_{2}\leq 1
. Applying (
3.3
) with
(\varrho,\theta)
replaced by
(\tau_{2}\varrho,\tau_{2}\theta)
and
\tau
replaced by
\frac{\tau_{1}}{\tau_{2}}
, we obtain

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{M}}}(\tau_{1})

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{2}{\bm{\mathsf{M}}}(\tau_{2})+\frac{C}{(\tau_{2}-\tau_{1})^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}(N+p)}}\bigg[\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q}}}+C\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}}+C\mu\varrho,

where

[\tau,1]\ni s\mapsto{\bm{\mathsf{M}}}(s):=\sup_{Q_{s\varrho,s\theta}}|u|.

Invoking Lemma
2.1
, we obtain for every
\tau\in[\frac{1}{2},1)
,

\displaystyle\sup_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}|u|

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg[\frac{1}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q}}}+C\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}}+C\mu\varrho,

where
\bm{\lambda}_{s}=N(p-2)+ps
, and
C=C(N,p,C_{o},C_{1},q,{\bm{\mathsf{r}}})
.
This proves the claimed quantitative
L^{\infty}
-estimate in the case
2<{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2

The case

\bm{p^{\prime}+2\leq{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}
.
The guiding idea is again to control the individual terms on the right-hand side of the energy inequality by means of the

L^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
-integral of the truncated function

(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n})_{+}
. More precisely, using (
3.11
) with
(a,b)=(p^{\prime}+p,{\bm{\mathsf{r}}})
, we get

(3.36)

{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{1}\leq\frac{2^{(n+1)r}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-(p^{\prime}+p)}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t=2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+p}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n},

where we set

\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}:=\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

Using (
3.11
) with
(a,b)=(p^{\prime}+2,{\bm{\mathsf{r}}})
we get

(3.37)

{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{2}\leq\frac{2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-(p^{\prime}+2)}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t=2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}.

In addition, applying (
3.12
) with
a={\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
we have

(3.38)

\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|\leq\frac{2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}\iint_{Q_{n}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n}\big)_{+}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t=2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}.

For the
\mu
-term
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{3}
we estimate it like (
3.3
) using Hölder’s inequality with exponents
p
and
p^{\prime}
, together with (
3.36
) and (
3.38
) and obtain

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\mu^{p}2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}.

In the same vein we estimate
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{4}
(the

{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}
-term) like (
3.3
), using the local boundedness of
u
, Hölder’s inequality with exponents
q
and
\frac{q}{q-1}
and the measure bound (
3.38
), and obtain

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{4}\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{p}2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}(1-\frac{1}{q})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}.

We estimate the
{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}
-Term
{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{5}
like (
3.3
), using the local boundedness of
u
, Hölder’s inequality with exponents
qp^{\prime}
and
(1-\frac{1}{qp^{\prime}})^{-1}
,
and the measure estimate (
3.38
),
to obtain

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{I}}}_{5}\leq|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{qp}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}(1-\frac{1}{q})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}.

Using (
3.36
), (
3.37
), (
3.38
), together with the bounds for the
\mu
,
{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}
, and
{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}
-terms,
we infer

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)^{p^{\prime}+2}\zeta_{n}^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad\leq C2^{(n+1)({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+p)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\bigg[\frac{1}{(1-\tau)^{p}\varrho^{p}}\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2-p}}+\frac{1}{(1-\tau)\theta}+\frac{\mu^{p}}{\sf{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2}}\bigg]\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}

\displaystyle\quad\phantom{\leq\,}+C2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}(1-\frac{1}{q})}\Big[\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{p}+|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{qp}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{p^{\prime}+1}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}\Big]\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\frac{C2^{(n+1)({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+p)}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}\bigg[\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{2-p}}\Big(1+\frac{\mu^{p}\varrho^{p}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p}}\Big)+1\bigg]\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}+C2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}(1-\frac{1}{q})}{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}},

where
{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}
was defined in (
3.19
).
Exactly as in the case
2<{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2
, we fix the truncation level
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}

so that (
3.20
) holds. With this choice the right-hand side of the energy inequality simplifies, and we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}

\displaystyle\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)^{p^{\prime}+2}\zeta_{n}^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

(3.39)

\displaystyle\leq C2^{(n+1)({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+p)}\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}+{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}\bigg].

This is the same energy estimate as (
3.3
) except an immaterial change of exponent of
2
.
Next, we apply the parabolic Sobolev embedding (Proposition 3.1 in Chapter I of
[
4
]
) with

(v,q,p,m)=\Big(\big(|u|-k_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}},p,{\bm{\mathsf{m}}},{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}\Big),

where
{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}
and
{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}
are given in (
3.22
).
The embedding yields

(3.40)

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n}}

\displaystyle\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq C_{\rm Sob}^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}\big|\nabla\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]\big|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]

\displaystyle\qquad\,\,\,\cdot\bigg[\sup_{t\in(-\theta_{n},0]}\int_{B_{n}\times\{t\}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta^{p^{\prime}}_{n}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big[\mbox{right-hand side of \eqref{est:en-prel-r}}\Big]^{1+\frac{p}{N}}.

Noting that
{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}<p^{\prime}+2\leq{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
, we first raise the exponent from
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
to
p^{\prime}{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}

by Hölder’s inequality, then lower it to

p^{\prime}{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}

at the cost of a factor
\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}
, and finally use the measure estimate (
3.38
) together with (
3.40
). This gives

\displaystyle\iint_{Q_{n+1}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\leq\iint_{Q_{n}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}\zeta_{n}\big]^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\quad\leq\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}\zeta_{n}\big]^{p^{\prime}{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{1-\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\bigg[\iint_{Q_{n}}\big[\big(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\big)_{+}^{p^{\prime}}\zeta_{n}^{p^{\prime}}\big]^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}\big|Q_{n}\cap\{|u|>{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n+1}\}\big|^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\Big[\mbox{right-hand side of \eqref{est:en-prel-r}}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1+\frac{p}{N})}\Big[2^{(n+1){\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}\Big]^{\frac{1}{p}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}2^{(n+1)({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+p)(1+\frac{p}{N})}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{\frac{1}{p}}\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}+{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1-\frac{1}{q}}\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1+\frac{p}{N})}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}2^{(n+1)({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+p)(1+\frac{p}{N})}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}\frac{N+p}{N}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}\bigg[\frac{\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{\frac{1}{q}}}{(1-\tau)^{p}\theta}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{p^{\prime}+2}}\bigg]^{\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\frac{N+p}{N}}

\displaystyle\quad\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n+1}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}\frac{N+p}{N}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}\bigg[\frac{\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}^{q}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\frac{N+p}{qp}},

where

{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}:=2^{({\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+p)(1+\frac{p}{N})}.

Dividing both sides by
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}
gives

\displaystyle\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n+1}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}\frac{N+p}{N}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,\,\,}\cdot\bigg[\frac{\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}^{q}}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\frac{N+p}{qp}}.

Passing to mean values in

\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n}
,
\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{n+1}
and
\widetilde{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}}_{o}
, we obtain the recursive inequality

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n+1}|Q_{o}|^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}\frac{N+p}{N}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,\,\,}\cdot\bigg[\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{R}}}^{q}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\frac{N+p}{qp}},

where

{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}:=\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{n}}(|u|-{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}_{n})_{+}^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

As in the case
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2
, a judicious choice of the truncation level
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}

allows us to streamline the right–hand side of the recursive inequality. The bracket splits into three contributions – coming from
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}
, the
{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}
–term, and the
{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}
–term:

\Big[\dots\Big]=\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}}{(1-\tau)^{qp}\theta^{q}}+\frac{\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{qp^{\prime}}\|{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}\|_{qp,Q_{o}}^{qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}+\frac{|Q_{o}|^{\frac{1}{p}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{q(p^{\prime}+1)}\|{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}\|_{qp^{\prime},Q_{o}}^{q}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}

We now choose
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
sufficiently large to satisfy the three conditions in (
3.24
).
Consequently, the bracket is controlled by

\frac{\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}-q}(1-\tau)^{N+p-qp}}{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}}.

Using this control, the recursion simplifies to

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\bigg[\frac{|Q_{o}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,\,\,}\cdot{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{m}}}\frac{N+p}{N}+\frac{N}{p}(p-2)\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\frac{N+p}{qp}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n+1}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}.

Here, the exponent of

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
can be simplified. In fact, by (
3.22
),

\mbox{exponent of ${\bm{\mathsf{k}}}$}={\bm{\mathsf{q}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}+\frac{N}{p}(2-p)\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\Big(1-\frac{N+p}{qp}\Big).

Consequently,

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n+1}

\displaystyle\leq C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}\bigg[\frac{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\bigg]^{\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{n}{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}^{1+\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}(1-\frac{N+p}{qp})}.

Before proceeding with the iteration, we revisit the constraints (
3.24
) imposed on
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
. Solving them for

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
, the first condition in (
3.24
) yields an analogue of (
3.3
), that is

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}}\geq\frac{|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

whereas the second and third conditions give exactly (
3.3
) and (
3.3
) respectively.
Set

\alpha:=\frac{p}{N}\frac{1}{p^{\prime}}\Big(1-\frac{N+p}{qp}\Big),\quad{\sf C}:=C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\bigg[\frac{|Q_{o}|{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(2-p)}}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\bigg]^{\alpha}.

Notice that
\alpha>0
since
q>\frac{N+p}{p}
.
To apply Lemma
2.2
with
({\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n},{\bm{\mathsf{b}}},{\sf C},\alpha)
we need

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{o}

\displaystyle\leq{\sf C}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{-\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}}.

Equivalently,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{\frac{\bm{\mathfrak{q}}-{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{\alpha}}}{(C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}})^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{\frac{1}{\alpha^{2}}}}\frac{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}{|Q_{o}|}{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{N}{p}(p-2)+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}.

Since
\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}>0
(otherwise the claim is trivial), we may solve the preceding condition for
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
. This gives another condition on
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
, that is

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}\geq\frac{\big(C{\bm{\mathsf{b}}}^{1+\frac{1}{\alpha}}\big)^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}|Q_{o}|\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}\,\theta^{\frac{N+p}{p}}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t,

or equivalently,

{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}^{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}\geq\frac{C|B_{1}|\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t.

By Lemma
2.2
we have
{\bm{\mathsf{Y}}}_{n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. In particular,

\sup_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}|u|\leq{\bm{\mathsf{k}}},

provided
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
is chosen large as required above. Hence we fix
{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}
by

\displaystyle{\bm{\mathsf{k}}}

\displaystyle=\max\Bigg\{\bigg[\frac{|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{1-\frac{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}}\bigg[\frac{C|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{1}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}}\bigg[|B_{1}|\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}^{1-\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{q}}{\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}}\bigg[|B_{1}|^{p^{\prime}}\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\bigg]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q(p^{\prime}+2)}}},

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\,\,\,\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}},\mu\varrho\Bigg\}.

The bound can be refined via interpolation and the argument is essentially identical to the case
2<{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2
.
Indeed, apply Young’s inequality with conjugate exponents

s=\frac{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}{\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}},\qquad s^{\prime}=\frac{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}+\frac{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}-{\bm{\mathsf{q}}}}{\alpha}}{\frac{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}{p}},

to the second entry. This yields

\big[\text{second entry}\big]\leq\tfrac{1}{6}\|u\|_{\infty,Q_{o}}+C\left[\frac{|B_{1}|}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}},

The estimates of the third and forth entries are identical to (
3.33
) and (
3.34
) respectively.
Hence, for any
\tau\in[\frac{1}{2},1)
there holds

\displaystyle\sup_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}|u|

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{2}\sup_{Q_{\varrho,\theta}}|u|+\frac{C}{(1-\tau)^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{r}}(N+p)}}\left[\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\Bigg[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\Bigg]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\left[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q}}}+C\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}}+C\mu\varrho,

where
\bm{\lambda}_{s}=N(p-2)+ps
and
C=C(N,p,C_{o},C_{1},q,{\bm{\mathsf{r}}})
. The remainder of the argument is identical to the case
2<{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}<p^{\prime}+2
(up to harmless changes of constants). In particular, repeating the interpolation yields the same quantitative local
L^{\infty}
-bound, so the proof in the regime
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}\geq p^{\prime}+2
is complete.

Remark 3.7

(Quantative
L^{\infty}
-estimate: the case
\frac{2N}{N+2}<p\leq 2
)
.

In our notion of weak solution we assume

u\in C^{0}\big([0,T];L^{2}(E,\mathbb{R}^{k})\big)\cap L^{p}\big(0,T;W^{1,p}(E,\mathbb{R}^{k})\big).

By the parabolic Sobolev embedding this yields

u\in L^{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}_{\mathrm{loc}}(E_{T},\mathbb{R}^{k})\quad\text{with}\quad{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=\frac{p(N+2)}{N}>2.

Moreover,

\bm{\lambda}_{2}:=N(p-2)+2p=(N+2)p-2N>0.

Hence the structural condition
\bm{\lambda}_{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}>0
is met already with
{\bm{\mathsf{r}}}=2
, and the quantitative scheme developed for the sub-critical range
1<p\leq\tfrac{2N}{N+2}
(reverse Hölder + interpolation/iteration) applies verbatim. In particular, for any cylinder
Q_{o}:=Q_{\varrho,\theta}\Subset E_{T}
, and for any
\tau\in[\frac{1}{2},1)
we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{Q_{\tau\varrho,\tau\theta}}|u|

\displaystyle\leq C\left[\frac{1}{(1-\tau)^{N+p}}\Big(\frac{\varrho^{p}}{\theta}\Big)^{\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|u|^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right]^{\frac{p}{(N+2)p-2N}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\left[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{q-\frac{N}{p}}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho\,{\bm{\mathsf{F}}}|^{qp}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right]^{\frac{p}{\bm{\lambda}_{2q}}}

\displaystyle\phantom{\leq\,}+C\left[\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{p^{\prime}(q-\frac{N}{p})}\mathchoice{\vbox{\hbox{$\textstyle-\!-$}}\kern-8.47218pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-5.86113pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-4.56949pt}{\vbox{\hbox{$\scriptscriptstyle-\!-$}}\kern-3.81949pt}\!\iint_{Q_{o}}|\varrho^{p}{\bm{\mathsf{f}}}|^{qp^{\prime}}\,\mathrm{d}x\mathrm{d}t\right]^{\frac{p}{p^{\prime}\bm{\lambda}_{q}}}+C\Big(\frac{\theta}{\varrho^{p}}\Big)^{\frac{1}{2-p}}+C\mu\varrho,

where
q>\tfrac{N+p}{p}
as in the assumptions.
The dependence of the constant is of the same form as in the sub-critical case, namely
C=C(N,p,C_{o},C_{1},q)
.

4.
Reverse Hölder-type inequality

From here on, i.e. both in §
4
and in §
5
, we assume
\mu=0
in the structure conditions (
1.2
).
For
\lambda>0
and
z_{o}=(x_{o},t_{o})
, recall that we set

(4.1)

Q_{\varrho}^{(\lambda)}(z_{o}):=B_{\varrho}^{(\lambda)}(x_{o})\times\Lambda_{\varrho}(t_{o}),

where

B_{\varrho}^{(\lambda)}(x_{o}):=\big\{x\in\mathbb{R}^{N}\colon\ |x-x_{o}|<\lambda^{\frac{p-2}{2}}\varrho\big\},\quad\Lambda_{\varrho}(t_{o}):=(t_{o}-\varrho^{2},t_{o}+\varrho^{2}).

The parameter
\lambda
encodes the dimension of solutions and will restore the homogeneity of forthcoming estimates.

4.1.
Energy inequality

The proof of the following energy inequality is standard and can for instance be inferred from
[
15
, Lemma 3.2]
.

Lemma 4.1
.

Let
p>1
. Then, there exists a constant
C=C(N,p,C_{o},C_{1})
such that whenever
u
is a weak solution to (
1.1
) in
E_{T}
in the sense of Definition
2.5
with (
1.2
),
Q_{\varrho}^{(\lambda)}(z_{o})\subseteq E_{T}
,
r\in[\tfrac{1}{2}\varrho,\varrho)
, and
a\in\mathbb{R}^{k}
,
then the following energy estimate holds

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\Lambda_{r}(t_{o})}
